382|def build_partbatch(c0, types, empty, special, placements, N=19, K=8, Q=6):
383|    """M2 batch of one-column placements: selector s_i per placement, clause OR s_i; the
384|    first special column c* has exact label counts (binomial, as in build()) and, guarded by
385|    s_i, its A rows -> 0, B rows -> 1, P rows -> Q-1 and the labels 0, 1, Q-1 excluded for all
386|    other rows. Symmetry: P0 triple-label fixing; first-occurrence order among the triple
387|    labels of c* and among equal-count labels of the free special columns; lexicographic
388|    order between consecutive identical FREE special columns (column-major lex-leader).
389|    UNSAT iff every placement subcase is UNSAT."""
390|    placements = [pl[0] if len(pl) == 1 else pl for pl in placements]
391|    deg = [0] * c0
392|    for t in types:
393|        for x in t:
394|            deg[x] += 1
395|    member = [set(t) for t in types]
396|    for x in range(c0):
397|        member += [{x} for _ in range(4 - deg[x])]
398|    member += [set() for _ in range(empty)]
399|    assert len(member) == N
400|    profs = [[4] + [3] * (Q - 1)] * c0 + [sorted(p, reverse=True) for p in special]
401|    assert all(p == [4, 4] + [3] * (Q - 3) + [2] for p in profs[c0:])
402|    PAIR = Q - 1
403|    cs = c0
404|    v = lambda r, c, s: 1 + K * Q * r + Q * c + s  # noqa: E731
405|    B = Builder(N * K * Q)
406|    cl = B.cl
407|    for r in range(N):
408|        for c in range(K):
409|            cl.append([v(r, c, s) for s in range(Q)])
410|            for s, t in itertools.combinations(range(Q), 2):
411|                cl.append([-v(r, c, s), -v(r, c, t)])
412|            if c < c0:
413|                cl.append([v(r, c, 0)] if c in member[r] else [-v(r, c, 0)])
414|    for c in range(K):
415|        for s in range(Q):
416|            if c < c0 and s == 0:
417|                continue
418|            for sub in itertools.combinations(range(N), profs[c][s] + 1):
419|                cl.append([-v(r, c, s) for r in sub])
420|    sels = [B.new() for _ in placements]
421|    cl.append(list(sels))
422|    for s_i, (A, Bl, P) in zip(sels, placements):
423|        role = {}
424|        for r in A:
425|            role[r] = 0
426|        for r in Bl:
427|            role[r] = 1
428|        for r in P:
429|            role[r] = PAIR
430|        for r in range(N):
431|            if r in role:
432|                cl.append([-s_i, v(r, cs, role[r])])
433|            else:
434|                for s in (0, 1, PAIR):
435|                    cl.append([-s_i, -v(r, cs, s)])
436|    for c1, c2 in itertools.combinations(range(K), 2):
437|        for a in range(Q):
438|            for b in range(Q):
439|                for r, r2 in itertools.combinations(range(N), 2):
440|                    cl.append([-v(r, c1, a), -v(r, c2, b), -v(r2, c1, a), -v(r2, c2, b)])
441|    U = {}
442|    for r, r2 in itertools.combinations(range(N), 2):
443|        u = B.new()
444|        U[(r, r2)] = u
445|        wit = []
446|        for c in range(K):
447|            for s in range(Q):
448|                a = B.new()
449|                cl.append([-a, v(r, c, s)])
450|                cl.append([-a, v(r2, c, s)])
451|                wit.append(a)
452|        cl.append(wit + [u])
453|    ns = K - c0
454|    for r in range(N):
455|        lits = [U[(min(r, x), max(r, x))] for x in range(N) if x != r]
456|        target = (N - 1) - 2 * c0 - ns - len(member[r])
457|        for c in range(c0, K):
458|            for s in range(Q):
459|                lits += [v(r, c, s)] * (profs[c][s] - 2)
460|        o = B.totalizer(lits)
461|        if target < 0 or target > len(o):
462|            cl.append([])
463|            continue
464|        if target >= 1:
465|            cl.append([o[target - 1]])
466|        if target < len(o):
467|            cl.append([-o[target]])
468|    for c in range(c0):
469|        Qc = {r for r in range(N) if c in member[r]}
470|        best = None
471|        for d in range(c0):
472|            if d == c:
473|                continue
474|            S_ = [r for r in range(N) if d in member[r] and r not in Qc]
475|            if best is None or len(S_) > len(best):
476|                best = S_
477|        if best:
478|            for lab, r in enumerate(sorted(best), start=1):
479|                cl.append([v(r, c, lab)])
480|    for c in range(c0, K):
481|        if c == cs:
482|            prs = [(s, s + 1) for s in range(2, Q - 2)]
483|        else:
484|            prs = [(s, s + 1) for s in range(Q - 1) if profs[c][s] == profs[c][s + 1]]
485|        for s, s2 in prs:
486|            for r in range(N):
487|                cl.append([-v(r, c, s2)] + [v(r2, c, s) for r2 in range(r)])
488|    for c in range(cs + 1, K - 1):
489|        c2 = c + 1
490|        eq_prev = None
491|        for r in range(N):
492|            for k in range(1, Q):
493|                for s in range(k, Q):
494|                    cl.append(([-eq_prev] if eq_prev else []) + [-v(r, c, s)] + [v(r, c2, t) for t in range(k, Q)])
495|            if r < N - 1:
496|                e = B.new()
497|                for s in range(Q):
498|                    cl.append(([-eq_prev] if eq_prev else []) + [-v(r, c, s), -v(r, c2, s), e])
499|                eq_prev = e
500|    return B.nv, cl
501|
502|
503|def build_v2(c0, types, empty, special, N=19, K=8, Q=6):
504|    """M2 encoder version 2 (adds row symmetry breaking). Same clause families as build()
505|    except the symmetry breaking, which is now entirely lex-leader for ONE row-major reading
506|    order (rows top-down, columns left to right):
507|      * P0 columns: first-occurrence order of the triple labels 1..Q-1 (replaces the label
508|        fixing of build(), which is not a lex-leader constraint);
509|      * special columns: first-occurrence order among equal-count labels;
510|      * identical consecutive special columns: column read top-down lexicographically
511|        non-decreasing (the row-major lex-leader constraint of the column swap);
512|      * rows with identical P0 membership (same type), consecutive in the row order:
513|        row r lexicographically <= row r' (the lex-leader constraint of the transposition),
514|        encoded with order literals ge(r,c,k) <-> value >= k and prefix-equality literals.
515|    The lex-least array of each orbit under the group generated by these symmetries satisfies
516|    all of them."""
517|    deg = [0] * c0
518|    for t in types:
519|        for x in t:
520|            deg[x] += 1
521|    member = [set(t) for t in types]
522|    for x in range(c0):
523|        member += [{x} for _ in range(4 - deg[x])]
524|    member += [set() for _ in range(empty)]
525|    assert len(member) == N
526|    profs = [[4] + [3] * (Q - 1)] * c0 + [sorted(p, reverse=True) for p in special]
527|    v = lambda r, c, s: 1 + K * Q * r + Q * c + s  # noqa: E731
528|    B = Builder(N * K * Q)
529|    cl = B.cl
530|    for r in range(N):
531|        for c in range(K):
532|            cl.append([v(r, c, s) for s in range(Q)])
533|            for s, t in itertools.combinations(range(Q), 2):
534|                cl.append([-v(r, c, s), -v(r, c, t)])
535|            if c < c0:
536|                cl.append([v(r, c, 0)] if c in member[r] else [-v(r, c, 0)])
537|    for c in range(K):
538|        for s in range(Q):
539|            if c < c0 and s == 0:
540|                continue
541|            for sub in itertools.combinations(range(N), profs[c][s] + 1):
542|                cl.append([-v(r, c, s) for r in sub])
543|    for c1, c2 in itertools.combinations(range(K), 2):
544|        for a in range(Q):
545|            for b in range(Q):
546|                for r, r2 in itertools.combinations(range(N), 2):
547|                    cl.append([-v(r, c1, a), -v(r, c2, b), -v(r2, c1, a), -v(r2, c2, b)])
548|    U = {}
549|    for r, r2 in itertools.combinations(range(N), 2):
550|        u = B.new()
551|        U[(r, r2)] = u
552|        wit = []
553|        for c in range(K):
554|            for s in range(Q):
555|                a = B.new()
556|                cl.append([-a, v(r, c, s)])
557|                cl.append([-a, v(r2, c, s)])
558|                wit.append(a)
559|        cl.append(wit + [u])
560|    ns = K - c0
561|    for r in range(N):
562|        lits = [U[(min(r, x), max(r, x))] for x in range(N) if x != r]
563|        target = (N - 1) - 2 * c0 - ns - len(member[r])
564|        for c in range(c0, K):
565|            for s in range(Q):
566|                n = profs[c][s]
567|                if n >= 2:
568|                    lits += [v(r, c, s)] * (n - 2)
569|                else:
570|                    lits.append(-v(r, c, s))
571|                    target += 1
572|        o = B.totalizer(lits)
573|        if target < 0 or target > len(o):
574|            cl.append([])
575|            continue
576|        if target >= 1:
577|            cl.append([o[target - 1]])
578|        if target < len(o):
579|            cl.append([-o[target]])
580|    # first-occurrence order (P0 triple labels; equal-count special labels)
581|    for c in range(K):
582|        if c < c0:
583|            prs = [(s, s + 1) for s in range(1, Q - 1)]
584|        else:
585|            prs = [(s, s + 1) for s in range(Q - 1) if profs[c][s] == profs[c][s + 1]]
586|        for s, s2 in prs:
587|            for r in range(N):
588|                cl.append([-v(r, c, s2)] + [v(r2, c, s) for r2 in range(r)])
589|    # identical consecutive special columns: top-down lex
590|    for c in range(c0, K - 1):
591|        c2 = c + 1
592|        if profs[c] != profs[c2]:
593|            continue
594|        eq_prev = None
595|        for r in range(N):
596|            for k in range(1, Q):
597|                for s in range(k, Q):
598|                    cl.append(([-eq_prev] if eq_prev else []) + [-v(r, c, s)] + [v(r, c2, t) for t in range(k, Q)])
599|            if r < N - 1:
600|                e = B.new()
601|                for s in range(Q):
602|                    cl.append(([-eq_prev] if eq_prev else []) + [-v(r, c, s), -v(r, c2, s), e])
603|                eq_prev = e
604|    # rows with identical type, consecutive: row lex (columns left to right)
605|    for r in range(N - 1):
606|        r2 = r + 1
607|        if member[r] != member[r2] or len(member[r]) >= 2:
608|            continue
609|        eq_prev = None
610|        for c in range(K):
611|            for k in range(1, Q):
612|                for s in range(k, Q):
613|                    cl.append(([-eq_prev] if eq_prev else []) + [-v(r, c, s)] + [v(r2, c, t) for t in range(k, Q)])
614|            if c < K - 1:
615|                e = B.new()
616|                for s in range(Q):
617|                    cl.append(([-eq_prev] if eq_prev else []) + [-v(r, c, s), -v(r2, c, s), e])
618|                eq_prev = e
619|    return B.nv, cl
620|

===== FILE lane/src/extend_p1q.py (sha256 bd2292c3031bfeda73d7b59f7c1b4b2351103eaeb99197a910a021c194c71b35, 158 lines; shown 1-158) =====
1|#!/usr/bin/env python3
2|"""[extend_p1q: generalization of extend_p1.py to q symbols with N = 3q+1, the P1 analogue
3|being (4,4,3,...,3,2); for q=6 identical behaviour. Written separately so that extend_p1.py,
4|which the running A3 batch calls, stays unchanged.]
5|Amendment A3: finer split of an A2 skeleton case whose special columns are all
6|P1 = (4,4,3,3,3,2). Enumerates every placement of the special columns' two size-4 blocks
7|(A, B) and the size-2 block (P), i.e. the 'full skeleton', subject only to necessary
8|conditions, then deduplicates up to isomorphism.
9|
10|Necessary conditions used (all from validity, see README A3):
11|  * blocks of one column are disjoint; |A|=|B|=4, |P|=2;
12|  * two rows already agreeing (sharing a P0 quad or an earlier special block) are never put
13|    in a common block;
14|  * row budget: |T(r)| + w(r) <= 2 and >= 2 - L, w(r) = sum over special columns of e,
15|    e = +1 (A or B), 0 (triple), -1 (P); pruned with the bound that each remaining column
16|    changes w by at most +1 / at least -1.
17|Symmetry reduction during search (orbit representatives under row permutations that fix the
18|P0 skeleton and all choices made so far): within each class of rows with identical history,
19|only lowest-index rows are chosen. Both orders of the two size-4 blocks are enumerated. Final
20|deduplication by networkx isomorphism of colored incidence graphs (covers Aut of the P0
21|skeleton, swapping A/B, and permuting identical special columns).
22|Usage (research venv): extend_p1.py SPEC_JSON OUT_JSON
23|"""
24|import itertools
25|import json
26|import os
27|import sys
28|
29|import networkx as nx
30|
31|HERE = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
32|sys.path.insert(0, HERE)
33|from encode_skel import rows_of  # noqa: E402
34|
35|
36|
37|def main():
38|    spec = json.load(open(sys.argv[1]))
39|    c0, L, special = spec["c0"], spec["L"], spec["special"]
40|    N = spec.get("N", 19)
41|    q = (N - 1) // 3
42|    P1 = [4, 4] + [3] * (q - 3) + [2]
43|    assert all(list(p) == P1 for p in special)
44|    ns = len(special)
45|    m = int(sys.argv[3]) if len(sys.argv) > 3 else ns  # number of special columns to fix
46|    assert 1 <= m <= ns
47|    N = spec.get("N", 19)
48|    rows = rows_of(c0, spec["skeleton"], N)
49|    T = [len(t) for t in rows]
50|    agree0 = set()
51|    for v in range(c0):
52|        members = [r for r in range(N) if v in rows[r]]
53|        for a, b in itertools.combinations(members, 2):
54|            agree0.add((a, b))
55|    results = []
56|
57|    def choose(avail_sorted, k, hist, agree):
58|        """k-subsets of avail rows, pairwise not agreeing, taking a prefix of each class of
59|        rows with identical history (orbit representatives)."""
60|        cls = {}
61|        for r in avail_sorted:
62|            cls.setdefault(hist[r], []).append(r)
63|        order = sorted(cls.values(), key=lambda rs: rs[0])
64|
65|        def rec(ci, need, chosen):
66|            if need == 0:
67|                yield tuple(sorted(chosen))
68|                return
69|            if ci == len(order):
70|                return
71|            rs = order[ci]
72|            for take in range(min(need, len(rs)), -1, -1):
73|                pick = rs[:take]
74|                ok = all((min(a, b), max(a, b)) not in agree for a, b in itertools.combinations(pick, 2))
75|                ok = ok and all((min(a, b), max(a, b)) not in agree for a in pick for b in chosen)
76|                if ok:
77|                    yield from rec(ci + 1, need - take, chosen + pick)
78|
79|        yield from rec(0, k, [])
80|
81|    def budget_ok(w, cols_left):
82|        for r in range(N):
83|            if T[r] + w[r] - cols_left > 2:
84|                return False
85|            if T[r] + w[r] + cols_left < 2 - L:
86|                return False
87|        return True
88|
89|    def col(ci, w, agree, hist, placed):
90|        if ci == m:
91|            if budget_ok(w, ns - m):
92|                results.append([[list(b) for b in p] for p in placed])
93|            return
94|        left = ns - ci - 1
95|        allr = list(range(N))
96|        candA = [r for r in allr if T[r] + w[r] + 1 - left <= 2]
97|        for A in choose(candA, 4, hist, agree):
98|            hA = list(hist)
99|            for r in A:
100|                hA[r] = hist[r] + ("A%d" % ci,)
101|            agA = set(agree) | {(a, b) for a, b in itertools.combinations(A, 2)}
102|            candB = [r for r in candA if r not in A]
103|            for B in choose(candB, 4, hA, agA):
104|                hB = list(hA)
105|                for r in B:
106|                    hB[r] = hA[r] + ("B%d" % ci,)
107|                agB = agA | {(a, b) for a, b in itertools.combinations(B, 2)}
108|                candP = [r for r in allr if r not in A and r not in B and T[r] + w[r] - 1 + left >= 2 - L]
109|                for P in choose(candP, 2, hB, agB):
110|                    w2 = list(w)
111|                    for r in A + B:
112|                        w2[r] += 1
113|                    for r in P:
114|                        w2[r] -= 1
115|                    if not budget_ok(w2, left):
116|                        continue
117|                    hP = list(hB)
118|                    for r in P:
119|                        hP[r] = hB[r] + ("P%d" % ci,)
120|                    for r in allr:
121|                        if r not in A and r not in B and r not in P:
122|                            hP[r] = hP[r] + ("T%d" % ci,)
123|                    agP = agB | {(min(P), max(P))}
124|                    col(ci + 1, w2, agP, tuple(hP), placed + [(A, B, P)])
125|
126|    hist0 = tuple((rows[r],) for r in range(N))
127|    col(0, [0] * N, agree0, hist0, [])
128|    reps = {}
129|    nm = lambda a, b: a["kind"] == b["kind"]  # noqa: E731
130|    for pl in results:
131|        G = nx.Graph()
132|        for v in range(c0):
133|            G.add_node(("q", v), kind="P0quad")
134|        for r in range(N):
135|            G.add_node(("r", r), kind="row")
136|            for v in rows[r]:
137|                G.add_edge(("r", r), ("q", v))
138|        for ci, (A, B, P) in enumerate(pl):
139|            G.add_node(("c", ci), kind="P1col")
140|            for name, blk in (("A", A), ("B", B), ("P", P)):
141|                G.add_node((name, ci), kind="pair" if name == "P" else "squad")
142|                G.add_edge((name, ci), ("c", ci))
143|                for r in blk:
144|                    G.add_edge(("r", r), (name, ci))
145|        key = nx.weisfeiler_lehman_graph_hash(G, node_attr="kind", iterations=4)
146|        bucket = reps.setdefault(key, [])
147|        if any(nx.is_isomorphic(G, H, node_match=nm) for H, _ in bucket):
148|            continue
149|        bucket.append((G, pl))
150|    full = [pl for b in reps.values() for _, pl in b]
151|    json.dump({"source": os.path.basename(sys.argv[1]), "raw": len(results), "num_full": len(full),
152|               "full": full}, open(sys.argv[2], "w"))
153|    print(json.dumps({"source": os.path.basename(sys.argv[1]), "raw": len(results), "num_full": len(full)}))
154|
155|
156|if __name__ == "__main__":
157|    main()

===== FILE lane/src/extend_b.py (sha256 31924ed66099baf6f03436e6d51793549cd45e96416200aeea8c23af651a26d7, 142 lines; shown 1-142) =====
1|#!/usr/bin/env python3
2|"""Independent second enumerator (M2) of one-column placements for A2 cases whose special
3|columns are all P1 = (4,4,3,...,3,2); shares no code with extend_p1.py / extend_p1q.py.
4|
5|Input: a gen_skel_b skeleton (row types + #empty rows). Rows (M2 order): the listed types,
6|then 4-deg(v) private rows of each P0 column v, then the empty rows. Rows with identical P0
7|membership form a class; their rows are interchangeable. A placement of the first special
8|column = disjoint blocks A, B (4 rows) and P (2 rows), each block pairwise without a common
9|P0 quad, subject to the row budget with the remaining ns-1 free special columns:
10|  |T(r)| + e(r) - (ns-1) <= 2  and  |T(r)| + e(r) + (ns-1) >= 2 - L
11|(e = +1 in A/B, 0 in a triple, -1 in P). Up to row symmetry a placement is a choice, for each
12|class, of how many of its rows go to A, B, P (a class with a nonempty P0 set contributes at
13|most one row per block). Placements are then identified under the automorphism group of the
14|skeleton (P0 column permutations preserving the type multiset, found by brute force over all
15|c0! permutations) and the A/B swap; one canonical representative per orbit is output.
16|Usage: extend_b.py SKEL_B_JSON INDEX OUT_JSON
17|"""
18|import itertools
19|import json
20|import sys
21|
22|
23|def main():
24|    path, idx, out = sys.argv[1], int(sys.argv[2]), sys.argv[3]
25|    d = json.load(open(path))
26|    sk = d["skeletons"][idx]
27|    c0, L = d["c0"], d["L"]
28|    q = len(d["profiles"][0])
29|    P0 = [4] + [3] * (q - 1)
30|    special = [p for p in d["profiles"] if list(p) != P0]
31|    ns = len(special)
32|    assert all(sorted(p, reverse=True) == [4, 4] + [3] * (q - 3) + [2] for p in special)
33|    types = [tuple(t) for t in sk["types"]]
34|    deg = [0] * c0
35|    for t in types:
36|        for v in t:
37|            deg[v] += 1
38|    member = [frozenset(t) for t in types]
39|    for v in range(c0):
40|        member += [frozenset([v])] * (4 - deg[v])
41|    member += [frozenset()] * sk["empty"]
42|    N = len(member)
43|    assert N == 3 * q + 1
44|    classes = []  # (member set, [rows])
45|    for r, ms in enumerate(member):
46|        for cl in classes:
47|            if cl[0] == ms:
48|                cl[1].append(r)
49|                break
50|        else:
51|            classes.append((ms, [r]))
52|    K = len(classes)
53|
54|    def ok_row(t, e):
55|        return t + e - (ns - 1) <= 2 and t + e + (ns - 1) >= 2 - L
56|
57|    # per class allowed (a, b, p) counts
58|    opts = []
59|    for ms, rows in classes:
60|        t = len(ms)
61|        cap = len(rows)
62|        per_block = 1 if ms else cap
63|        o = []
64|        for a in range(min(per_block, cap) + 1):
65|            for b in range(min(per_block, cap - a) + 1):
66|                for p in range(min(per_block, cap - a - b) + 1):
67|                    rest = cap - a - b - p
68|                    good = True
69|                    if a + b and not ok_row(t, 1):
70|                        good = False
71|                    if p and not ok_row(t, -1):
72|                        good = False
73|                    if rest and not ok_row(t, 0):
74|                        good = False
75|                    if good:
76|                        o.append((a, b, p))
77|        opts.append(o)
78|    results = []
79|
80|    def compatible(sel, k, need):
81|        # blocks pairwise without common P0 quad: classes with nonempty sets in the same block
82|        # must have disjoint sets
83|        return True
84|
85|    def rec(k, sel, sa, sb, sp):
86|        if sa > 4 or sb > 4 or sp > 2:
87|            return
88|        if k == K:
89|            if sa == 4 and sb == 4 and sp == 2:
90|                results.append(tuple(sel))
91|            return
92|        ms = classes[k][0]
93|        for a, b, p in opts[k]:
94|            bad = False
95|            for j in range(k):
96|                aj, bj, pj = sel[j]
97|                if ms and classes[j][0] and (ms & classes[j][0]):
98|                    if (a and aj) or (b and bj) or (p and pj):
99|                        bad = True
100|                        break
101|            if bad:
102|                continue
103|            sel.append((a, b, p))
104|            rec(k + 1, sel, sa + a, sb + b, sp + p)
105|            sel.pop()
106|
107|    rec(0, [], 0, 0, 0)
108|    # automorphisms of the skeleton
109|    tset = sorted(tuple(sorted(t)) for t in types)
110|    auts = []
111|    for perm in itertools.permutations(range(c0)):
112|        if sorted(tuple(sorted(perm[v] for v in t)) for t in types) == tset:
113|            auts.append(perm)
114|    cls_sets = [tuple(sorted(ms)) for ms, _ in classes]
115|    reps = {}
116|    for sel in results:
117|        best = None
118|        for perm in auts:
119|            mapped = []
120|            for (ms, _), (a, b, p) in zip(classes, sel):
121|                mapped.append((tuple(sorted(perm[v] for v in ms)), a, b, p))
122|            for swap in (False, True):
123|                key = tuple(sorted((m, b, a, p) if swap else (m, a, b, p) for m, a, b, p in mapped))
124|                if best is None or key < best:
125|                    best = key
126|        reps.setdefault(best, sel)
127|    placements = []
128|    for key, sel in sorted(reps.items()):
129|        A, B, P = [], [], []
130|        for (ms, rows), (a, b, p) in zip(classes, sel):
131|            A += rows[:a]
132|            B += rows[a:a + b]
133|            P += rows[a + b:a + b + p]
134|        placements.append([sorted(A), sorted(B), sorted(P)])
135|    json.dump({"source": path, "index": idx, "raw_class_assignments": len(results), "automorphisms": len(auts),
136|               "num_placements": len(placements), "placements": placements}, open(out, "w"))
137|    print(json.dumps({"index": idx, "raw": len(results), "aut": len(auts), "num_placements": len(placements)}))
138|
139|
140|if __name__ == "__main__":
141|    main()
142|

===== FILE lane/src/encode_full2.py (sha256 bb8d134e56f925caa84d44b9ceb8e0a986f987790135ab063be17573829a52c9, 128 lines; shown 1-128) =====
1|#!/usr/bin/env python3
2|"""Encoder FULL2 = encode_full.py plus EXACT leave counts (Amendment A3-exact): z(r,r') is
3|forced false when rows r, r' agree in some column (clauses -z | -x[r][c][s] | -x[r'][c][s]), so
4|z is exactly "agree nowhere", and row r misses AT LEAST l(r) rows (besides at most l(r)).
5|Valid: row r agrees with exactly sum_c (n_{c,A[r][c]} - 1) = 18 - l(r) other rows (the agreeing
6|sets of different columns are disjoint), so it misses exactly l(r) rows.
7|Original docstring of encode_full.py:
8|Encoder FULL (Amendment A3, method M1 family): completion of one full skeleton.
9|
10|Columns 0..c0-1 = P0 columns (symbol 0 = quad, fixed; symbols 1..5 = triples, count 3).
11|Columns c0.. = P1 columns (4,4,3,3,3,2): symbol 0 = A, 1 = B, 5 = P fixed; symbols 2,3,4 =
12|triples, count 3. Every non-triple cell is fixed, so each row's leave degree is known:
13|l(r) = 2 - |T(r)| - w(r). Constraints: EO cells; fixed blocks; triple counts; AMO agreements;
14|row r misses at most l(r) rows and at most L pairs miss in total (equality then follows by
15|counting); value precedence among triple symbols of every column; lex(row i) <= lex(row j)
16|for rows with identical fixed pattern (lex-leader of the row transposition, row-major order).
17|"""
18|import encode_e1
19|from encode_skel import rows_of
20|
21|
22|def build(spec):
23|    c0, L = spec["c0"], spec["L"]
24|    N, k, q = spec.get("N", 19), spec.get("k", 8), spec.get("q", 6)
25|    rows = rows_of(c0, spec["skeleton"], N)
26|    placed = spec["placed"]  # per special column [A, B, P]
27|    ns = len(placed)
28|    assert c0 + ns == k
29|    fixed = {}  # (r, c) -> symbol for non-triple cells
30|    triple_syms = {}
31|    for c in range(c0):
32|        for r in range(N):
33|            if c in rows[r]:
34|                fixed[(r, c)] = 0
35|        triple_syms[c] = [1, 2, 3, 4, 5]
36|    w = [0] * N
37|    for i, (A, B, P) in enumerate(placed):
38|        c = c0 + i
39|        for r in A:
40|            fixed[(r, c)] = 0
41|            w[r] += 1
42|        for r in B:
43|            fixed[(r, c)] = 1
44|            w[r] += 1
45|        for r in P:
46|            fixed[(r, c)] = 5
47|            w[r] -= 1
48|        triple_syms[c] = [2, 3, 4]
49|    leave = [2 - len(rows[r]) - w[r] for r in range(N)]
50|    assert all(0 <= x <= L for x in leave) and sum(leave) == 2 * L, leave
51|    F = encode_e1.CNF()
52|    X = [[[F.var(("x", r, c, s)) for s in range(q)] for c in range(k)] for r in range(N)]
53|    for r in range(N):
54|        for c in range(k):
55|            F.add(list(X[r][c]))
56|            for s in range(q):
57|                for t in range(s + 1, q):
58|                    F.add([-X[r][c][s], -X[r][c][t]])
59|            if (r, c) in fixed:
60|                F.add([X[r][c][fixed[(r, c)]]])
61|            else:
62|                for s in range(q):
63|                    if s not in triple_syms[c]:
64|                        F.add([-X[r][c][s]])
65|    for c in range(k):
66|        for s in triple_syms[c]:
67|            F.exactly([X[r][c][s] for r in range(N)], 3)
68|    Z = {}
69|    for r in range(N):
70|        for r2 in range(r + 1, N):
71|            ys = []
72|            ms = []
73|            for c in range(k):
74|                y = F.new()
75|                ys.append(y)
76|                for s in range(q):
77|                    F.add([-X[r][c][s], -X[r2][c][s], y])
78|                    m = F.new()
79|                    F.add([-m, X[r][c][s]])
80|                    F.add([-m, X[r2][c][s]])
81|                    ms.append(m)
82|            for i in range(k):
83|                for j in range(i + 1, k):
84|                    F.add([-ys[i], -ys[j]])
85|            z = F.new()
86|            Z[(r, r2)] = z
87|            F.add([z] + ms)
88|    for (r, r2), z in Z.items():
89|        for c in range(k):
90|            for s in range(q):
91|                F.add([-z, -X[r][c][s], -X[r2][c][s]])
92|    for r in range(N):
93|        zr = [Z[(min(r, x), max(r, x))] for x in range(N) if x != r]
94|        F.at_most(zr, leave[r])
95|        F.at_most([-z for z in zr], len(zr) - leave[r])
96|    F.at_most(list(Z.values()), L)
97|    for c in range(k):
98|        syms = triple_syms[c]
99|        for a, b in zip(syms, syms[1:]):
100|            P_ = [F.new() for _ in range(N)]
101|            F.add([-P_[0], X[0][c][a]])
102|            F.add([-X[0][c][a], P_[0]])
103|            for r in range(1, N):
104|                F.add([-P_[r - 1], P_[r]])
105|                F.add([-X[r][c][a], P_[r]])
106|                F.add([-P_[r], P_[r - 1], X[r][c][a]])
107|            F.add([-X[0][c][b]])
108|            for r in range(1, N):
109|                F.add([-X[r][c][b], P_[r - 1]])
110|    pattern = [tuple(fixed.get((r, c), -1) for c in range(k)) for r in range(N)]
111|    nlex = 0
112|    for r in range(N):
113|        nxt = [r2 for r2 in range(r + 1, N) if pattern[r2] == pattern[r]]
114|        if not nxt:
115|            continue
116|        r2 = nxt[0]
117|        nlex += 1
118|        E = [F.new() for _ in range(k - 1)]
119|        for c in range(k):
120|            guard = [] if c == 0 else [-E[c - 1]]
121|            for s in range(q):
122|                for t in range(s):
123|                    F.add(guard + [-X[r][c][s], -X[r2][c][t]])
124|            if c < k - 1:
125|                for s in range(q):
126|                    F.add(guard + [-X[r][c][s], -X[r2][c][s], E[c]])
127|    return F, X, {"leave": leave, "row_lex_pairs": nlex}
128|

===== FILE lane/src/encode_partq.py (sha256 6702283c4fe03d687f19503a07318494bb7917ea92a311fe20de284d662df9d9, 145 lines; shown 1-145) =====
1|#!/usr/bin/env python3
2|"""[encode_partq: generalization of encode_part.py to q symbols (N = 3q+1): P1 analogue
3|(4,4,3,...,3,2) with A -> 0, B -> 1, P -> q-1, triples 2..q-2.]
4|Encoder PART (Amendment A3', method M1 family): an A2 skeleton case of a multiset whose
5|special columns are all P1 = (4,4,3,3,3,2), with the blocks (A, B, P) of the first m special
6|columns fixed (one extension produced by `extend_p1.py SPEC OUT m`), the other special
7|columns free. Same constraint families as encode_skel, adapted:
8|  * fixed special columns: A -> 0, B -> 1, P -> 5 fixed, other rows restricted to 2..4;
9|  * value precedence: P0 columns 1..5; fixed special columns 2..4 only (the A/B order is
10|    fixed by the placement); free special columns among equal counts as in encode_skel;
11|  * row lex only between rows with identical fixed pattern (P0 set T and roles in the fixed
12|    columns) -- such rows are interchangeable in the subcase;
13|  * column lex only between consecutive identical FREE special columns.
14|All breaking constraints are lex-leader for one row-major order (P0 columns, fixed special
15|columns, free special columns).
16|"""
17|import encode_e1
18|from encode_skel import rows_of
19|
20|
21|def build(spec):
22|    c0, skel, special = spec["c0"], spec["skeleton"], [list(p) for p in spec["special"]]
23|    N, k, q = spec.get("N", 19), spec.get("k", 8), spec.get("q", 6)
24|    placed = spec["placed"]
25|    m = len(placed)
26|    assert all(p == [4, 4] + [3] * (q - 3) + [2] for p in special)
27|    PAIR = q - 1
28|    TRI = list(range(2, q - 1))
29|    profs = [[4] + [3] * (q - 1)] * c0 + special
30|    rows = rows_of(c0, skel, N)
31|    fixed = {}
32|    for r in range(N):
33|        for c in range(c0):
34|            if c in rows[r]:
35|                fixed[(r, c)] = 0
36|    for i, (A, B, P) in enumerate(placed):
37|        c = c0 + i
38|        for r in A:
39|            fixed[(r, c)] = 0
40|        for r in B:
41|            fixed[(r, c)] = 1
42|        for r in P:
43|            fixed[(r, c)] = PAIR
44|    F = encode_e1.CNF()
45|    X = [[[F.var(("x", r, c, s)) for s in range(q)] for c in range(k)] for r in range(N)]
46|    for r in range(N):
47|        for c in range(k):
48|            F.add(list(X[r][c]))
49|            for s in range(q):
50|                for t in range(s + 1, q):
51|                    F.add([-X[r][c][s], -X[r][c][t]])
52|            if c < c0:
53|                F.add([X[r][c][0]] if c in rows[r] else [-X[r][c][0]])
54|            elif c < c0 + m:
55|                if (r, c) in fixed:
56|                    F.add([X[r][c][fixed[(r, c)]]])
57|                else:
58|                    for s in (0, 1, PAIR):
59|                        F.add([-X[r][c][s]])
60|    for c in range(k):
61|        for s in range(q):
62|            if c < c0 and s == 0:
63|                continue
64|            if c0 <= c < c0 + m and s in (0, 1, PAIR):
65|                continue
66|            F.exactly([X[r][c][s] for r in range(N)], profs[c][s])
67|    for r in range(N):
68|        for r2 in range(r + 1, N):
69|            ys = []
70|            for c in range(k):
71|                y = F.new()
72|                ys.append(y)
73|                for s in range(q):
74|                    F.add([-X[r][c][s], -X[r2][c][s], y])
75|            for i in range(k):
76|                for j in range(i + 1, k):
77|                    F.add([-ys[i], -ys[j]])
78|    kap = sum(n * (n - 1) // 2 for p in profs for n in p)
79|    L = N * (N - 1) // 2 - kap
80|    assert L == spec["L"]
81|    wmax = [max(p) - 1 for p in profs]
82|    for r in range(N):
83|        F.weighted_at_most([[(X[r][c][s], profs[c][s] - 1) for s in range(q)] for c in range(k)], N - 1)
84|        F.weighted_at_most([[(X[r][c][s], wmax[c] - (profs[c][s] - 1)) for s in range(q)]
85|                            for c in range(k)], sum(wmax) - (N - 1 - L))
86|    zs = []
87|    for r in range(N):
88|        for r2 in range(r + 1, N):
89|            z = F.new()
90|            zs.append(z)
91|            cl = [z]
92|            for c in range(k):
93|                for s in range(q):
94|                    mm = F.new()
95|                    F.add([-mm, X[r][c][s]])
96|                    F.add([-mm, X[r2][c][s]])
97|                    cl.append(mm)
98|            F.add(cl)
99|    F.at_most(zs, L)
100|    for c in range(k):
101|        if c < c0:
102|            pairs = [(s, s + 1) for s in range(1, q - 1)]
103|        elif c < c0 + m:
104|            pairs = list(zip(TRI, TRI[1:]))
105|        else:
106|            pairs = [(s, s + 1) for s in range(q - 1) if profs[c][s] == profs[c][s + 1]]
107|        for s, s2 in pairs:
108|            P_ = [F.new() for _ in range(N)]
109|            F.add([-P_[0], X[0][c][s]])
110|            F.add([-X[0][c][s], P_[0]])
111|            for r in range(1, N):
112|                F.add([-P_[r - 1], P_[r]])
113|                F.add([-X[r][c][s], P_[r]])
114|                F.add([-P_[r], P_[r - 1], X[r][c][s]])
115|            F.add([-X[0][c][s2]])
116|            for r in range(1, N):
117|                F.add([-X[r][c][s2], P_[r - 1]])
118|    pattern = [tuple(fixed.get((r, c), -1) for c in range(c0 + m)) for r in range(N)]
119|    for r in range(N):
120|        nxt = [r2 for r2 in range(r + 1, N) if pattern[r2] == pattern[r]]
121|        if not nxt:
122|            continue
123|        r2 = nxt[0]
124|        E = [F.new() for _ in range(k - 1)]
125|        for c in range(k):
126|            guard = [] if c == 0 else [-E[c - 1]]
127|            for s in range(q):
128|                for t in range(s):
129|                    F.add(guard + [-X[r][c][s], -X[r2][c][t]])
130|            if c < k - 1:
131|                for s in range(q):
132|                    F.add(guard + [-X[r][c][s], -X[r2][c][s], E[c]])
133|    for c in range(c0 + m, k - 1):
134|        c2 = c + 1
135|        E = [F.new() for _ in range(N - 1)]
136|        for r in range(N):
137|            guard = [] if r == 0 else [-E[r - 1]]
138|            for s in range(q):
139|                for t in range(s):
140|                    F.add(guard + [-X[r][c][s], -X[r][c2][t]])
141|            if r < N - 1:
142|                for s in range(q):
143|                    F.add(guard + [-X[r][c][s], -X[r][c2][s], E[r]])
144|    return F, X, {"m": m}

===== FILE lane/src/encode_partbatch.py (sha256 ee4496cc927406ad3f0bff10a00c6c04cb6e34080424e2798bbc2af8c31012e9, 148 lines; shown 1-148) =====
1|#!/usr/bin/env python3
2|"""Encoder PARTBATCH (A3'' batching, method M1 family): one CNF for a BATCH of one-column
3|placements of an all-P1 A2 case (placements from extend_p1q.py with m = 1).
4|
5|Selector t_i per placement i, clause (t_1 | ... | t_b). The first special column c* = c0 is
6|encoded like a free special column with exact label counts (A -> 0, B -> 1 count 4, triples
7|2..q-2 count 3, P -> q-1 count 2) and, guarded by t_i, the cells of placement i fixed
8|(positive guarded units for its A, B, P rows; guarded exclusions of labels 0, 1, q-1 for the
9|other rows). Symmetry: value precedence among the triple labels of c* only (the A/B order is
10|part of the placement); guarded by t_i, row lex between rows with identical fixed pattern
11|(P0 set and role in c* under placement i); column lex between consecutive identical free
12|special columns; the rest exactly as encode_partq.py. The CNF is UNSAT iff every placement
13|subcase of the batch is UNSAT (each satisfying assignment satisfies some t_i together with
14|that subcase's constraints, and conversely).
15|"""
16|import encode_e1
17|from encode_skel import rows_of
18|
19|
20|def build(spec, placements):
21|    # extend_p1q with m = 1 yields [[A, B, P]] per placement; accept [A, B, P] too
22|    placements = [pl[0] if len(pl) == 1 else pl for pl in placements]
23|    c0, skel, special = spec["c0"], spec["skeleton"], [list(p) for p in spec["special"]]
24|    N, k, q = spec.get("N", 19), spec.get("k", 8), spec.get("q", 6)
25|    PAIR = q - 1
26|    TRI = list(range(2, q - 1))
27|    assert all(p == [4, 4] + [3] * (q - 3) + [2] for p in special)
28|    profs = [[4] + [3] * (q - 1)] * c0 + special
29|    rows = rows_of(c0, skel, N)
30|    cs = c0  # the column whose placement is batched
31|    F = encode_e1.CNF()
32|    X = [[[F.var(("x", r, c, s)) for s in range(q)] for c in range(k)] for r in range(N)]
33|    for r in range(N):
34|        for c in range(k):
35|            F.add(list(X[r][c]))
36|            for s in range(q):
37|                for t in range(s + 1, q):
38|                    F.add([-X[r][c][s], -X[r][c][t]])
39|            if c < c0:
40|                F.add([X[r][c][0]] if c in rows[r] else [-X[r][c][0]])
41|    for c in range(k):
42|        for s in range(q):
43|            if c < c0 and s == 0:
44|                continue
45|            F.exactly([X[r][c][s] for r in range(N)], profs[c][s])
46|    sel = [F.new() for _ in placements]
47|    F.add(list(sel))
48|    for t_i, (A, B, P) in zip(sel, placements):
49|        role = {}
50|        for r in A:
51|            role[r] = 0
52|        for r in B:
53|            role[r] = 1
54|        for r in P:
55|            role[r] = PAIR
56|        for r in range(N):
57|            if r in role:
58|                F.add([-t_i, X[r][cs][role[r]]])
59|            else:
60|                for s in (0, 1, PAIR):
61|                    F.add([-t_i, -X[r][cs][s]])
62|    for r in range(N):
63|        for r2 in range(r + 1, N):
64|            ys = []
65|            for c in range(k):
66|                y = F.new()
67|                ys.append(y)
68|                for s in range(q):
69|                    F.add([-X[r][c][s], -X[r2][c][s], y])
70|            for i in range(k):
71|                for j in range(i + 1, k):
72|                    F.add([-ys[i], -ys[j]])
73|    kap = sum(n * (n - 1) // 2 for p in profs for n in p)
74|    L = N * (N - 1) // 2 - kap
75|    assert L == spec["L"]
76|    wmax = [max(p) - 1 for p in profs]
77|    for r in range(N):
78|        F.weighted_at_most([[(X[r][c][s], profs[c][s] - 1) for s in range(q)] for c in range(k)], N - 1)
79|        F.weighted_at_most([[(X[r][c][s], wmax[c] - (profs[c][s] - 1)) for s in range(q)]
80|                            for c in range(k)], sum(wmax) - (N - 1 - L))
81|    zs = []
82|    for r in range(N):
83|        for r2 in range(r + 1, N):
84|            z = F.new()
85|            zs.append(z)
86|            cl = [z]
87|            for c in range(k):
88|                for s in range(q):
89|                    mm = F.new()
90|                    F.add([-mm, X[r][c][s]])
91|                    F.add([-mm, X[r2][c][s]])
92|                    cl.append(mm)
93|            F.add(cl)
94|    F.at_most(zs, L)
95|    for c in range(k):
96|        if c < c0:
97|            pairs = [(s, s + 1) for s in range(1, q - 1)]
98|        elif c == cs:
99|            pairs = list(zip(TRI, TRI[1:]))
100|        else:
101|            pairs = [(s, s + 1) for s in range(q - 1) if profs[c][s] == profs[c][s + 1]]
102|        for s, s2 in pairs:
103|            P_ = [F.new() for _ in range(N)]
104|            F.add([-P_[0], X[0][c][s]])
105|            F.add([-X[0][c][s], P_[0]])
106|            for r in range(1, N):
107|                F.add([-P_[r - 1], P_[r]])
108|                F.add([-X[r][c][s], P_[r]])
109|                F.add([-P_[r], P_[r - 1], X[r][c][s]])
110|            F.add([-X[0][c][s2]])
111|            for r in range(1, N):
112|                F.add([-X[r][c][s2], P_[r - 1]])
113|    for t_i, (A, B, P) in zip(sel, placements):
114|        role = {}
115|        for r in A:
116|            role[r] = 0
117|        for r in B:
118|            role[r] = 1
119|        for r in P:
120|            role[r] = PAIR
121|        pattern = [(rows[r], role.get(r, -1)) for r in range(N)]
122|        for r in range(N):
123|            nxt = [r2 for r2 in range(r + 1, N) if pattern[r2] == pattern[r]]
124|            if not nxt:
125|                continue
126|            r2 = nxt[0]
127|            E = [F.new() for _ in range(k - 1)]
128|            for c in range(k):
129|                guard = [-t_i] + ([] if c == 0 else [-E[c - 1]])
130|                for s in range(q):
131|                    for t in range(s):
132|                        F.add(guard + [-X[r][c][s], -X[r2][c][t]])
133|                if c < k - 1:
134|                    for s in range(q):
135|                        F.add(guard + [-X[r][c][s], -X[r2][c][s], E[c]])
136|    for c in range(cs + 1, k - 1):
137|        c2 = c + 1
138|        E = [F.new() for _ in range(N - 1)]
139|        for r in range(N):
140|            guard = [] if r == 0 else [-E[r - 1]]
141|            for s in range(q):
142|                for t in range(s):
143|                    F.add(guard + [-X[r][c][s], -X[r][c2][t]])
144|            if r < N - 1:
145|                for s in range(q):
146|                    F.add(guard + [-X[r][c][s], -X[r][c2][s], E[r]])
147|    return F, X, sel

===== FILE lane/src/summarize15.py (sha256 164238814d3f2b624956e0ab3636c761d79f9cfb25ebf9406bae034d1bb66114, 79 lines; shown 1-79) =====
1|#!/usr/bin/env python3
2|"""[Guard15 base records accepted identically; frozen completed cell cover13/14 is unchanged and never rerun. Output STATUS15.json.]
3|[Margin variant: aggregate14 base plus exact frozen cell cover; both prior13 and new14 records accepted under the same full predicate. Output STATUS14.json; base STATUS14_BASE.json.]

6|SHA, reconstructed subcase-CNF SHA/size, and full revision-13 predicate. The summary file is never trusted. A miss
7|leaves the original case open; any SAT record is listed even if malformed. Output STATUS12.json; STATUS11 remains
8|the base aggregation without this cell-cover route.
9|"""
10|import contextlib
11|import hashlib
12|import io
13|import json
14|import os
15|
16|import aggregate15 as B
17|
18|LANE = B.LANE
19|TAG = 'ms06_s00468'
20|BASE_SHA = '137407b6532fea61a682467507462e146cef6dd546b9a496a6bd18ad0ceba994'
21|ROW, COL = 8, 6
22|
23|
24|def cell_cover(sats):
25|    base = open(os.path.join(LANE, 'cases19skel', 'cnf', TAG + '.cnf'), 'rb').read()
26|    if hashlib.sha256(base).hexdigest() != BASE_SHA:
27|        return {'all_six_checked': False, 'error': 'pinned base SHA mismatch'}
28|    head, sep, body = base.partition(b'\n')
29|    p, cnf, nv, nc = head.split()
30|    assert sep and (p, cnf) == (b'p', b'cnf') and base.endswith(b'\n')
31|    body_sha = hashlib.sha256(body).hexdigest()
32|    records = []
33|    for symbol in range(6):
34|        rel = os.path.join('cycle2', 'cell-cover', TAG, 'r08_c06', 's%02d.json' % symbol)
35|        r = B.jl(os.path.join(LANE, rel))
36|        if r and r.get('status') == 'SAT':
37|            sats.append(('cell-cover', rel))
38|        literal = 421 + symbol
39|        unit = ('%d 0\n' % literal).encode()
40|        expected = ('p cnf %d %d\n' % (int(nv), int(nc) + 1)).encode() + body + unit
41|        expected_sha = hashlib.sha256(expected).hexdigest()
42|        binding = bool(r and r.get('tag') == TAG and type(r.get('row')) is int and r['row'] == ROW
43|                       and type(r.get('column')) is int and r['column'] == COL
44|                       and type(r.get('symbol')) is int and r['symbol'] == symbol
45|                       and type(r.get('unit_literal')) is int and r['unit_literal'] == literal
46|                       and r.get('unit_clause') == unit.decode() and r.get('base_header') == head.decode()
47|                       and r.get('base_cnf_sha256') == BASE_SHA and r.get('base_body_sha256') == body_sha
48|                       and r.get('base_byte_identical') is True and r.get('exact_one_unit_append') is True
49|                       and r.get('base_bytes') == len(base) and r.get('cnf_sha256') == expected_sha
50|                       and r.get('cnf_bytes') == len(expected))
51|        checked = bool(binding and r.get('stream_check_revision') in (13, 14) and B.certified(r))
52|        records.append({'symbol': symbol, 'file': rel, 'unit_literal': literal, 'expected_cnf_sha256': expected_sha,
53|                        'binding_ok': binding, 'checked': checked})
54|    return {'tag': TAG, 'row': ROW, 'column': COL, 'base_cnf_sha256': BASE_SHA, 'records': records,
55|            'all_six_checked': len(records) == 6 and all(r['checked'] for r in records)}
56|
57|
58|def main():
59|    with contextlib.redirect_stdout(io.StringIO()):
60|        B.main()
61|    out = json.load(open(os.path.join(LANE, 'STATUS15_BASE.json')))
62|    cover = cell_cover(out['sat_results'])
63|    out['cell_cover_s00468'] = cover
64|    if cover['all_six_checked'] and TAG in out['M1_open_tags']:
65|        out['M1_open_tags'].remove(TAG)
66|        out['M1_open'] = len(out['M1_open_tags'])
67|        p = out['per_multiset']['ms06']
68|        p['M1_certified'] += 1
69|        p['M1_how']['cell-six-unit'] = 1
70|        if TAG not in out['M2_open_tags']:
71|            p['both'] += 1
72|    with open(os.path.join(LANE, 'STATUS15.json'), 'w') as f:
73|        json.dump(out, f, indent=1)
74|    print(json.dumps({k: v for k, v in out.items() if not k.endswith('_tags') and k != 'uncertified_list'}, indent=1))

===== FILE lane/src/aggregate15.py (sha256 efd16b878cc23f68cfcaa24442f65a41cd5694cb3c4284ba259dc9af833e9d13, 192 lines; shown 1-192) =====
1|#!/usr/bin/env python3
2|"""[Guard15 metadata tag accepted with exactly the same full predicate as guard14; retired10 excluded.]

15|drat_trim_verified and drat-trim 's ' lines exactly ['s VERIFIED']. Output STATUS7.json.

22|  * cycle-1 stored-proof checks (cases19skel/proofcheck, cases19q0 .check.json): 'ok' requires
23|    drat_trim_verified;

25|  * stream_check rev 5 results (kissat_drat / cadical_drat): 'checked' (drat-trim only).
26|UNCERTIFIED (listed, never used): CaDiCaL-LRAT results (cycle2/hard/*.json with a cadical
27|solver, *_r2.json, m2q0b/), any result whose acceptance rested on lrat-check alone.
28|M1 paths per A2 case: (a) pass-1 UNSAT + cycle-1 check; (b) pass-1 UNSAT + cycle-2 streamed
29|check; (c5) cycle2/hard5 kissat/cadical DRAT result with CNF SHA = pass 1; (d) A3 full split
30|covered by f/_exact/_r3 results; (e) A3'/A3'' covered by f/batch/_r3 results.
31|M2 per case (matched by compare-map): cycle2/m2skel_drat result, or cycle2/m2part_drat batches
32|covering all extend_b placements. E0: M1 cycle-1 checks; M2 m2q0c (rev 5).
33|SAT outcomes in any path are listed. Open lists are complete.

36|import json
37|import os

39|
40|LANE = os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))

43|def jl(p):
44|    try:
45|        return json.load(open(p))
46|    except (OSError, ValueError):
47|        return None
48|
49|
50|def certified(r):
51|    if not r or r.get("status") != "UNSAT":
52|        return False
53|    if "lratstrict_verified" in r:
54|        return False
55|    if r.get("stream_check_revision") == 8:
56|        return False
57|    if r.get("stream_check_revision") in (11, 12, 13, 14, 15):
58|        return bool(r.get("checked")) and bool(r.get("drat_trim_verified")) and \
59|            (r.get("drat_trim") or {}).get("s_lines") == ["s VERIFIED"] and r.get("replay_ok") is True and \
60|            r.get("reader_ok") is True and not r.get("admission_denied") and not r.get("aborted_by_sigterm")
61|    if r.get("stream_check_revision") == 10:
62|        return False  # rev-10 records were retired (archive/rev10-superseded-20261017T160921); never counted
63|    if r.get("stream_check_revision") == 9:
64|        return bool(r.get("checked")) and bool(r.get("drat_trim_verified")) and \
65|            (r.get("drat_trim") or {}).get("s_lines") == ["s VERIFIED"]
66|    if r.get("stream_check_revision") == 5:
67|        return bool(r.get("checked")) and bool(r.get("drat_trim_verified"))
68|    return bool(r.get("drat_trim_verified")) and bool(r.get("checked"))
69|
70|
71|def split_cover(d, n, sats, label):
72|    cov = set()
73|    for f in glob.glob(os.path.join(d, "f*.json")):
74|        m = re.match(r".*/f(\d+)(_exact|_r2|_r3)?\.json$", f)
75|        if not m:
76|            continue
77|        r = jl(f)
78|        if r and r.get("status") == "SAT":
79|            sats.append((label, os.path.relpath(f, LANE)))
80|        if m.group(2) == "_r2":
81|            continue
82|        if certified(r):
83|            cov.add(int(m.group(1)))
84|    for f in glob.glob(os.path.join(d, "batch_*.json")):
85|        r = jl(f)
86|        if r and r.get("status") == "SAT":
87|            sats.append((label, os.path.relpath(f, LANE)))
88|        if f.endswith("_r2.json"):
89|            continue
90|        if certified(r):
91|            idx = r.get("indices") or []
92|            if "multiset" in r and len(idx) == 2:
93|                idx = list(range(idx[0], idx[1] + 1))
94|            cov.update(idx)
95|    return cov
96|
97|
98|def main():
99|    idx = json.load(open(os.path.join(LANE, "cases19skel", "index.json")))
100|    maps = {}

104|    per, open_m1, open_m2, sats, uncert = {}, [], [], [], []

109|            sats.append(("pass1", tag))

113|        if p1 and p1["status"] == "UNSAT" and pc and pc.get("ok") and pc.get("drat_trim_verified"):
114|            how = "a"
115|        elif p1 and p1["status"] == "UNSAT" and ps and ps.get("ok") and ps.get("drat_trim_verified") \
116|                and ps.get("cnf_matches_pass1_sha"):
117|            how = "b"

121|        if old:

126|        h5 = jl(os.path.join(LANE, "cycle2", "hard5", tag + ".json"))

128|            sats.append(("hard5", tag))
129|        if not how and certified(h5) and h5.get("cnf_matches_pass1_sha"):
130|            how = "c5"

132|            d = os.path.join(LANE, "cycle2", sub, tag)

139|                how = kind

141|                uncert.append(("r2 (CaDiCaL LRAT + lrat-check)", os.path.relpath(f, LANE)))
142|        m2 = None

146|                r = jl(os.path.join(LANE, "cycle2", sub, "ms%02d_b%05d.json" % (mi, bi)))
147|                if r and r.get("status") == "SAT":

149|                if not m2 and certified(r):

4|Revision 13 = revision 12 plus helper memory safeguards (REV11c proposal, 2026-10-17 ~16:5x; extend_p1q used
5|~4.7 GB on ms06_s00214): run_helper (1) takes an exclusive helper lock cycle2/tokens/helper, so at most one helper
6|runs in the lane; (2) refuses to start (AdmissionDenied "lane RSS headroom") unless the current lane RSS (one ps sum
7|over processes whose command line contains the lane path or an exact lane runner path) plus HELPER_RSS_CAP_KB is
8|at most LANE_RSS_LIMIT_KB (8 GiB); (3) samples the helper's RSS every second and, above HELPER_RSS_CAP_KB (5 GiB),
9|sends SIGTERM to that helper -- its own registered, not yet reaped Popen child, so the pid cannot have been reused --
10|then waits for it (quarantine if it survives). The result is recorded in LAST_HELPER (peak RSS, cap hit). Records
11|carry stream_check_revision 13. Revision-12 text follows.
12|Revision 12 = revision 11 plus an admission re-check INSIDE the bigmem lock, immediately before the checker
13|spawn (parent re-review ~16:1x: a stage could wait for the bigmem lock and then start drat-trim after a stop
14|file / disk-floor change). If not admitted there, the stage returns UNKNOWN (admission_denied) without
15|starting drat-trim. Records carry stream_check_revision 12. Revision-11 text follows.
16|Revision 11 of the online proof-checking helpers: the staged, token-gated pipeline of revision 10 with the
17|parent audit fixes (PA19StagedAudit, 2026-10-17 ~15:3x-15:4x):
18|
19|* No silent token release. A solver, checker or helper that survives SIGTERM + TERM_WAIT is QUARANTINED: a
20|  record cycle2/QUARANTINE-<runner pid>-<child pid>-<time>.json is written and the pipeline keeps holding its
21|  CPU token (and the bigmem lock, if held) while polling the survivor every 5 s, without further signals,
22|  until it exits by itself. Only then does the stage return (UNKNOWN). Helpers run through run_helper()
23|  (Popen + unbounded wait) under the token.
24|* Runner SIGTERM handling (install_sigterm_handler): sets ABORT; if the runner has registered live children
25|  (solver, checker, helper) it forwards SIGTERM to each of them and lets the stage finish as UNKNOWN (token
26|  released only after they have exited, quarantine otherwise); if it has none and is not in the middle of
27|  starting one (e.g. CNF construction in Python) it raises SystemExit so the stage is abandoned before any
28|  child is started. While a child is being started (SPAWNING) the handler does not abandon the stage; the
29|  new child is terminated right after registration. SIGTERM is never blocked around child creation (a blocked
30|  signal mask is inherited across fork/exec and made children unstoppable in a first smoke run).
31|* Proof replay binding: the hash/count of the proof fed to drat-trim are taken only over bytes whose write
32|  to drat-trim's stdin completed; the decompressor must reach the end of the single zstd frame; stdin must
33|  close without error; the reader of the solver's proof must reach EOF without error. Any failure ->
34|  replay_ok False -> not checked (UNKNOWN), even if drat-trim prints 's VERIFIED'.
35|* CPU accounting (parent decision 15:4x): one token covers the stage's solver or checker PLUS the runner's
36|  concurrent compression/decompression/feeding (or the runner's CNF construction, or one helper), i.e. at
37|  most 2 busy processes per token; the lane runs with COUNT <= 2 tokens (cycle2/tokens/COUNT).
38|* The token file records the holder (pid, time, stage) on acquisition, for the RSS watchdog.
39|* Admission re-checks (parent audit 15:5x): immediately AFTER every token acquisition and again before every
40|  CNF construction, helper start, solver start and checker start, admitted() checks the runner's ABORT flag,
41|  the runner's stop files (set_stop_files) and the 12e9-byte free-disk floor. A refused admission raises
42|  AdmissionDenied before the token acquisition returns / before construction or a helper, or ends the stage as
43|  UNKNOWN (admission_denied) before a solver/checker start; the token is released only when no owned child
44|  is alive (Token.__exit__ waits, quarantine otherwise).
45|Acceptance (checked=True) iff: solver exited on its own with rc 20; no proof cap hit; proof reader reached EOF
46|without error; drat-trim exited on its own (no quarantine) with rc 0; its 's ' lines, each stripped of '\\r',
47|are exactly ['s VERIFIED']; replay_ok (complete delivery, SHA-256 and length equal to the streamed proof).
48|Caps unchanged from revision 10 (raw proof 1200 MiB, compressed buffer 500 MiB including the final frame
49|tail, bigmem > 300 MiB, check budget <= 1200 s, solver limit as given). The proof caps are checked after
50|each read chunk of at most 1 MiB, so the buffers can exceed a cap by at most one chunk (<= 1 MiB raw and
51|its compressed output) plus zstd's internal state before the solver is stopped; they are admission/size
52|limits, not instantaneous RSS limits. The lane RAM guard is the aggregate watchdog (sampled every 2 s),
53|also not instantaneous; with COUNT <= 2 the margins (2 x (500 MiB buffer + solver + checker), checks of
54|proofs > 300 MiB serialized) are chosen to stay well below 8 GiB with room for sampling delay.

138|def spawn(cmd, stdin=False):
139|    """Popen + registration in ACTIVE. SIGTERM is NOT blocked (a blocked mask would be inherited by the child,
140|    which then could not be stopped -- found by smoke test L). Instead SPAWNING tells the handler not to abandon
141|    the stage while a child may exist unregistered; a SIGTERM that arrived meanwhile is honoured right after
142|    registration by terminating the new child."""
143|    SPAWNING[0] = True
144|    try:
145|        p = subprocess.Popen(cmd, stdin=subprocess.PIPE if stdin else subprocess.DEVNULL,
146|                             stdout=subprocess.PIPE, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT)
147|        with _alock:
148|            ACTIVE.add(p)
149|    finally:
150|        SPAWNING[0] = False
151|    if ABORT[0]:
152|        try:
153|            p.terminate()
154|        except OSError:
155|            pass
156|    return p
157|
158|
159|def write_json(path, obj):
160|    """record write with SIGTERM blocked (a pending SIGTERM is handled after the record is complete)"""
161|    old = signal.pthread_sigmask(signal.SIG_BLOCK, {signal.SIGTERM})
162|    try:
163|        with open(path, "w") as f:
164|            json.dump(obj, f, indent=1)
165|    finally:
166|        signal.pthread_sigmask(signal.SIG_SETMASK, old)
167|
168|
169|def unregister(p):
170|    with _alock:
171|        ACTIVE.discard(p)
172|
173|
174|def _collect(stream, sink):
175|    for line in iter(stream.readline, b""):
176|        sink.append(line)
177|    stream.close()
178|
179|
180|def _start(cmd, stdin=False):
181|    p = spawn(cmd, stdin)
182|    sink = []
183|    t = threading.Thread(target=_collect, args=(p.stdout, sink), daemon=True)
184|    t.start()
185|    return p, sink, t
186|
187|
188|def quarantine(p, name, events):
189|    t0 = time.time()
190|    q = {"time": time.strftime("%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%S%z"), "runner_pid": os.getpid(), "child_pid": p.pid, "child": name,
191|         "note": "child survived SIGTERM + TERM_WAIT; token (and bigmem lock if held) retained until it exits; no further signals"}
192|    qp = os.path.join(LANE, "cycle2", "QUARANTINE-%d-%d-%s.json" % (os.getpid(), p.pid, time.strftime("%Y%m%dT%H%M%S")))
193|    with open(qp, "w") as f:
194|        json.dump(q, f, indent=1)
195|    while p.poll() is None:
196|        time.sleep(5)
197|    events.append({"process": name, "pid": p.pid, "action": "quarantine-ended", "quarantine_seconds": round(time.time() - t0, 1),
198|                   "record": os.path.relpath(qp, LANE)})
199|    return "quarantined"
200|
201|
202|def stop_cooperatively(p, bound, events, name):
203|    """returns 'self' (exited on its own within bound), 'term' (exited after SIGTERM) or 'quarantined'
204|    (survived SIGTERM + TERM_WAIT and has exited since; it was waited for while the token was held)"""
205|    try:
206|        try:
207|            p.wait(timeout=max(1.0, bound))
208|            return "self" if not ABORT[0] or p.returncode >= 0 else "term"
209|        except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
210|            pass
211|        p.terminate()
212|        events.append({"process": name, "pid": p.pid, "action": "SIGTERM", "time": round(time.time(), 1)})
213|        try:
214|            p.wait(timeout=TERM_WAIT)
215|            return "term"
216|        except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
217|            events.append({"process": name, "pid": p.pid, "action": "survived-SIGTERM", "time": round(time.time(), 1)})
218|            return quarantine(p, name, events)
219|    finally:
220|        if p.poll() is not None:
221|            unregister(p)

227|LAST_HELPER = {}

365|def _fifos(wdir, names):
366|    wdir = "%s_%d_%d" % (wdir, os.getpid(), time.time_ns())
367|    os.makedirs(wdir, exist_ok=True)
368|    out = {}
369|    for n in names:
370|        p = os.path.join(wdir, n)
371|        os.mkfifo(p)
372|        out[n] = p
373|    return out
374|
375|
376|def _writer(path, data):
377|    try:
378|        with open(path, "wb") as f:
379|            f.write(data)
380|    except OSError:
381|        pass
382|
383|
384|def _rss_kb(pid):
385|    try:
386|        out = subprocess.run(["ps", "-o", "rss=", "-p", str(pid)], capture_output=True, text=True).stdout.strip()
387|        return int(out) if out else 0
388|    except (OSError, ValueError):
389|        return 0
390|
391|
392|def _drat_staged(cnf_source, wdir, limit, check_budget, solver_cmd, solver_name):
393|    """cnf_source: CNF bytes, or a callable returning them (built while the token is held)"""
394|    with Token(stage=solver_name) as tok:
395|        r = admitted_reason()
396|        if r:
397|            raise AdmissionDenied(r)
398|        t_enc = time.time()
399|        cnf_bytes = cnf_source() if callable(cnf_source) else cnf_source
400|        res = _drat_staged_locked(cnf_bytes, wdir, limit, check_budget, solver_cmd, solver_name)
401|        res["token"], res["token_wait_seconds"] = tok.name, tok.waited
402|        res["encode_seconds"] = round(res.pop("_t_solver_start") - t_enc, 2)
403|        res["aborted_by_sigterm"] = ABORT[0]
404|        if ABORT[0]:
405|            res["checked"] = False
406|        return res
407|
408|
409|def _drat_staged_locked(cnf_bytes, wdir, limit, check_budget, solver_cmd, solver_name):
410|    cb = int(min(check_budget, MAX_CHECK_BUDGET))
411|    F = _fifos(wdir, ["cnf_s", "cnf_d", "prf"])
412|    res = {"cnf_sha256": hashlib.sha256(cnf_bytes).hexdigest(), "cnf_bytes": len(cnf_bytes),
413|           "solver": "%s; binary DRAT staged (zstd-1 in memory) then drat-trim -i -t %d" % (
414|               " ".join(solver_cmd("CNF", "PROOF", limit)), cb),
415|           "solver_info": SOLVER_INFO[solver_name], "drat_trim_binary": "tools/drat-trim/drat-trim",
416|           "stream_check_revision": 15, "staged": True, "check_budget": cb,
417|           "fifo_dir": os.path.relpath(os.path.dirname(F["prf"]), LANE)}
418|    events = []
419|    hp, nraw = hashlib.sha256(), [0]
420|    zc = zstd.ZstdCompressor(level=1)
421|    chunks, zbytes, over, reader_ok = [], [0], [None], [False]
422|    sp_holder = {}
423|
424|    def reader():
425|        try:
426|            with open(F["prf"], "rb") as f:
427|                for b in iter(lambda: f.read(1 << 20), b""):
428|                    if over[0]:
429|                        continue  # drain so the solver is not blocked while it is being stopped
430|                    hp.update(b)
431|                    nraw[0] += len(b)
432|                    z = zc.compress(b)
433|                    if z:
434|                        chunks.append(z)
435|                        zbytes[0] += len(z)
436|                    if nraw[0] > MAX_RAW or zbytes[0] > MAX_ZBUF:
437|                        over[0] = "raw" if nraw[0] > MAX_RAW else "zbuf"
438|                        p = sp_holder.get("p")
439|                        if p is not None and p.poll() is None:
440|                            p.terminate()
441|                            events.append({"process": solver_name, "pid": p.pid,
442|                                           "action": "SIGTERM (proof size cap %s)" % over[0], "time": round(time.time(), 1)})
443|            if not over[0]:
444|                tail = zc.flush()
445|                if zbytes[0] + len(tail) > MAX_ZBUF:  # the frame tail counts against the cap too
446|                    over[0] = "zbuf-tail"
447|                    events.append({"proof_cap": "zbuf-tail", "zbuf_before_tail": zbytes[0], "tail_bytes": len(tail)})
448|                else:
449|                    if tail:
450|                        chunks.append(tail)
451|                        zbytes[0] += len(tail)
452|                    reader_ok[0] = True
453|        except Exception as e:  # noqa: BLE001 -- any reader failure makes the case UNKNOWN
454|            events.append({"reader_error": repr(e)[:200]})
455|
456|    r = admitted_reason()
457|    if r:  # before the solver start
458|        res.update({"_t_solver_start": time.time(), "status": "UNKNOWN", "admission_denied": r, "checked": False,
459|                    "drat_trim_verified": False, "events": events, "solver_out": ""})
460|        return res
461|    rt = threading.Thread(target=reader, daemon=True)
462|    ws = threading.Thread(target=_writer, args=(F["cnf_s"], cnf_bytes), daemon=True)
463|    rt.start()
464|    ws.start()
465|    t0 = time.time()
466|    res["_t_solver_start"] = t0
467|    sp, sp_out, sp_t = _start(solver_cmd(F["cnf_s"], F["prf"], limit))
468|    sp_holder["p"] = sp
469|    speak = [0]
470|    sdone = threading.Event()
471|
472|    def smon():
473|        while not sdone.wait(5):
474|            speak[0] = max(speak[0], _rss_kb(sp.pid))
475|
476|    threading.Thread(target=smon, daemon=True).start()
477|    s_stop = stop_cooperatively(sp, limit + GRACE, events, solver_name)
478|    sdone.set()
479|    rc = sp.returncode if s_stop == "self" else None
480|    res["solve_seconds"] = round(time.time() - t0, 2)
481|    res["solver_rc"] = rc
482|    res["solver_stop"] = s_stop
483|    res["solver_peak_rss_kb"] = speak[0]
484|    res["status"] = {10: "SAT", 20: "UNSAT"}.get(rc, "UNKNOWN")
485|    if over[0]:
486|        res["status"] = "UNKNOWN"
487|        res["proof_cap_hit"] = over[0]
488|    sp_t.join(timeout=10)
489|    res["solver_out"] = b"".join(sp_out).decode(errors="replace") if rc == 10 else ""
490|    rt.join(timeout=GRACE)
491|    ws.join(timeout=2)
492|    res["drat_sha256"] = hp.hexdigest()
493|    res["drat_bytes"] = nraw[0]
494|    res["zbuf_bytes"] = zbytes[0]
495|    res["reader_ok"] = reader_ok[0] and not rt.is_alive()
496|    r = admitted_reason()
497|    if r:
498|        res["admission_denied"] = r
499|    if res["status"] != "UNSAT" or not res["reader_ok"] or r:
500|        res.update({"events": events, "checked": False, "drat_trim_verified": False, "checker_interrupted": False,
501|                    "check_seconds": 0.0, "replay_ok": False})
502|        return res
503|    with BigLock(nraw[0] > BIG) as bl:
504|        r2 = admitted_reason()  # re-check after a possible bigmem wait, immediately before the checker spawn
505|        if r2:
506|            res.update({"admission_denied": r2, "events": events, "checked": False, "drat_trim_verified": False,
507|                        "checker_interrupted": False, "check_seconds": 0.0, "replay_ok": False, "bigmem_lock": bl.active})
508|            chunks.clear()
509|            return res
510|        t1 = time.time()
511|        dt, dt_out, dt_t = _start([DT, F["cnf_d"], "-i", "-t", str(cb)], stdin=True)
512|        wd = threading.Thread(target=_writer, args=(F["cnf_d"], cnf_bytes), daemon=True)
513|        wd.start()
514|        peak = [0]
515|        done = threading.Event()
516|        feed = {"ok": False, "sha": None, "n": 0, "error": None}
517|
518|        def feeder():
519|            hc, n = hashlib.sha256(), 0
520|            d = zstd.ZstdDecompressor()
521|            try:
522|                for z in chunks:
523|                    b = d.decompress(z)
524|                    if b:
525|                        dt.stdin.write(b)  # raises on a broken pipe; bytes are counted only after it returns
526|                        hc.update(b)
527|                        n += len(b)
528|                if not d.eof:
529|                    raise ValueError("zstd frame incomplete")
530|                dt.stdin.close()
531|                feed["ok"] = True
532|            except Exception as e:  # noqa: BLE001 -- any feed failure makes the case UNKNOWN
533|                feed["error"] = repr(e)[:200]
534|                try:
535|                    dt.stdin.close()
536|                except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001
537|                    pass
538|            feed["sha"], feed["n"] = hc.hexdigest(), n
539|
540|        def mon():
541|            while not done.wait(5):
542|                peak[0] = max(peak[0], _rss_kb(dt.pid))
543|
544|        ft = threading.Thread(target=feeder, daemon=True)
545|        mt = threading.Thread(target=mon, daemon=True)
546|        ft.start()
547|        mt.start()
548|        dt_stop = stop_cooperatively(dt, cb + GRACE, events, "drat-trim")
549|        done.set()
550|        dt_t.join(timeout=10)
551|        ft.join(timeout=30)
552|        wd.join(timeout=2)
553|        res["bigmem_lock"] = bl.active
554|    res["check_seconds"] = round(time.time() - t1, 2)
555|    res["drat_trim_peak_rss_kb"] = peak[0]
556|    res["replay_ok"] = bool(feed["ok"] and not ft.is_alive() and feed["sha"] == res["drat_sha256"] and feed["n"] == nraw[0])
557|    res["replay"] = {"ok": feed["ok"], "bytes": feed["n"], "sha256": feed["sha"], "error": feed["error"]}
558|    res["replay_sha256_matches"] = res["replay_ok"]
559|    dto = b"".join(dt_out).decode(errors="replace")
560|    s_lines = [l for l in (x.strip("\r") for x in dto.split("\n")) if l.startswith("s ")]
561|    res["drat_trim"] = {"stop": dt_stop, "rc": dt.returncode, "s_lines": s_lines, "says_verified": s_lines == ["s VERIFIED"],
562|                        "wall_seconds": res["check_seconds"]}
563|    res["checker_interrupted"] = dt_stop != "self"
564|    res["drat_trim_verified"] = (dt_stop == "self" and dt.returncode == 0 and res["drat_trim"]["says_verified"]
565|                                 and res["replay_ok"])
566|    res["events"] = events
567|    res["checked"] = bool(rc == 20 and not over[0] and res["reader_ok"] and res["drat_trim_verified"] and not ABORT[0])
568|    chunks.clear()
569|    return res
570|
571|
572|def _kissat_cmd(cnf, prf, limit):
573|    return [KISSAT, "-q", "-f", "--seed=1", "--time=%d" % int(limit), cnf, prf]
574|
575|
576|def _cadical_cmd(cnf, prf, limit):
577|    return [CADICAL, "-q", "-t", str(int(limit)), cnf, prf]
578|
579|
580|def kissat_staged(cnf_source, wdir, limit, check_budget=MAX_CHECK_BUDGET):
581|    return _drat_staged(cnf_source, wdir, limit, check_budget, _kissat_cmd, "kissat")
582|
583|
584|def cadical_staged(cnf_source, wdir, limit, check_budget=MAX_CHECK_BUDGET):
585|    return _drat_staged(cnf_source, wdir, limit, check_budget, _cadical_cmd, "cadical")

588|def cnf_bytes_of(F):
589|    parts = ["p cnf %d %d\n" % (F.nv, len(F.clauses))]
590|    parts.extend(" ".join(map(str, cl)) + " 0\n" for cl in F.clauses)

===== FILE lane/src/stream_check5.py (sha256 3060a9c064572b603e23fa82a9981f7d1e9d40a09d223c8f9f354c5ffde0c4ad, 169 lines; shown 1-169) =====
1|#!/usr/bin/env python3
2|"""Revision 5 of the online proof-checking helpers (earlier revisions kept unchanged).
3|
4|Change (parent finding 2026-10-17 ~12:20): lrat-check from the drat-trim repository accepts an
5|empty-clause LRAT step without a conflict (unsound), so it is no longer used at all. The only
6|acceptance criterion is drat-trim: it must exit ON ITS OWN with exit code 0 and print
7|'s VERIFIED' while checking the solver's binary DRAT proof streamed into its stdin (-i).
8|  kissat_drat : kissat 4.0.4 -q -f --seed=1 --time=LIMIT, binary DRAT
9|  cadical_drat: cadical 3.0.1 -q -t LIMIT (binary DRAT, --lrat=false)
10|Process policy as revision 4: native limits (solver; drat-trim -t LIMIT+BUDGET because its
11|clock starts before the streamed solve), bounded waits, SIGTERM only, survivors left running
12|and reported, interrupted checker => UNKNOWN. FIFO directories get a unique suffix
13|(pid + nanosecond time) so a rerun never reuses a FIFO of an earlier run.
14|"""
15|import hashlib
16|import os
17|import subprocess
18|import threading
19|import time
20|
21|HERE = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
22|LANE = os.path.dirname(HERE)
23|KISSAT = "/opt/homebrew/bin/kissat"
24|CADICAL = "/opt/homebrew/bin/cadical"
25|DT = os.path.join(LANE, "tools", "drat-trim", "drat-trim")
26|GRACE = 60
27|TERM_WAIT = 60
28|MAX_CHECK_BUDGET = 1200
29|
30|
31|def _fifos(wdir, names):
32|    wdir = "%s_%d_%d" % (wdir, os.getpid(), time.time_ns())
33|    os.makedirs(wdir, exist_ok=True)
34|    out = {}
35|    for n in names:
36|        p = os.path.join(wdir, n)
37|        os.mkfifo(p)
38|        out[n] = p
39|    return out
40|
41|
42|def _writer(path, data):
43|    try:
44|        with open(path, "wb") as f:
45|            f.write(data)
46|    except OSError:
47|        pass
48|
49|
50|def _forward(src_path, dst_file, h, counter):
51|    try:
52|        with open(src_path, "rb") as f:
53|            while True:
54|                b = f.read(1 << 16)
55|                if not b:
56|                    break
57|                h.update(b)
58|                counter[0] += len(b)
59|                try:
60|                    dst_file.write(b)
61|                except (OSError, ValueError):
62|                    pass
63|    finally:
64|        try:
65|            dst_file.close()
66|        except (OSError, ValueError):
67|            pass
68|
69|
70|def _collect(stream, sink):
71|    for line in iter(stream.readline, b""):
72|        sink.append(line)
73|    stream.close()
74|
75|
76|def _start(cmd, stdin=False):
77|    p = subprocess.Popen(cmd, stdin=subprocess.PIPE if stdin else subprocess.DEVNULL,
78|                         stdout=subprocess.PIPE, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT)
79|    sink = []
80|    t = threading.Thread(target=_collect, args=(p.stdout, sink), daemon=True)
81|    t.start()
82|    return p, sink, t
83|
84|
85|def stop_cooperatively(p, bound, events, name):
86|    try:
87|        p.wait(timeout=max(1.0, bound))
88|        return "self"
89|    except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
90|        pass
91|    p.terminate()
92|    events.append({"process": name, "pid": p.pid, "action": "SIGTERM", "time": round(time.time(), 1)})
93|    try:
94|        p.wait(timeout=TERM_WAIT)
95|        return "term"
96|    except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
97|        events.append({"process": name, "pid": p.pid, "action": "left-running", "time": round(time.time(), 1)})
98|        return "running"
99|
100|
101|def _drat_stream(cnf_bytes, wdir, limit, check_budget, solver_cmd, solver_name):
102|    cb = int(min(check_budget, MAX_CHECK_BUDGET))
103|    F = _fifos(wdir, ["cnf_s", "cnf_d", "prf"])
104|    res = {"cnf_sha256": hashlib.sha256(cnf_bytes).hexdigest(), "cnf_bytes": len(cnf_bytes),
105|           "solver": "%s; binary DRAT streamed to drat-trim -i -t %d" % (" ".join(solver_cmd(0, "CNF", "PROOF", limit)), int(limit) + cb),
106|           "stream_check_revision": 5, "check_budget": cb, "fifo_dir": os.path.relpath(os.path.dirname(F["prf"]), LANE)}
107|    events = []
108|    dt, dt_out, dt_t = _start([DT, F["cnf_d"], "-i", "-t", str(int(limit) + cb)], stdin=True)
109|    hp, npf = hashlib.sha256(), [0]
110|    fw = threading.Thread(target=_forward, args=(F["prf"], dt.stdin, hp, npf), daemon=True)
111|    wr = [threading.Thread(target=_writer, args=(F[n], cnf_bytes), daemon=True) for n in ("cnf_d", "cnf_s")]
112|    for t in [fw] + wr:
113|        t.start()
114|    t0 = time.time()
115|    sp, sp_out, sp_t = _start(solver_cmd(1, F["cnf_s"], F["prf"], limit))
116|    s_stop = stop_cooperatively(sp, limit + GRACE, events, solver_name)
117|    rc = sp.returncode if s_stop == "self" else None
118|    res["solve_seconds"] = round(time.time() - t0, 2)
119|    res["solver_rc"] = rc
120|    res["solver_stop"] = s_stop
121|    res["status"] = {10: "SAT", 20: "UNSAT"}.get(rc, "UNKNOWN")
122|    if s_stop != "running":
123|        sp_t.join(timeout=10)
124|    res["solver_out"] = b"".join(sp_out).decode(errors="replace") if rc == 10 else ""
125|    t1 = time.time()
126|    if s_stop == "running":
127|        res.update({"events": events, "checked": False, "drat_trim_verified": False, "checker_interrupted": True})
128|        return res
129|    unsat = res["status"] == "UNSAT"
130|    fw.join(timeout=(cb if unsat else GRACE))
131|    dt_stop = stop_cooperatively(dt, (cb + GRACE) if unsat else GRACE, events, "drat-trim")
132|    if dt_stop != "running":
133|        dt_t.join(timeout=10)
134|    for t in wr:
135|        t.join(timeout=2)
136|    res["check_seconds"] = round(time.time() - t1, 2)
137|    res["drat_sha256"] = hp.hexdigest()
138|    res["drat_bytes"] = npf[0]
139|    dto = b"".join(dt_out).decode(errors="replace")
140|    res["drat_trim"] = {"stop": dt_stop, "rc": dt.returncode if dt_stop != "running" else None,
141|                        "says_verified": "s VERIFIED" in dto, "says_timeout": "s TIMEOUT" in dto}
142|    res["checker_interrupted"] = dt_stop != "self"
143|    res["drat_trim_verified"] = dt_stop == "self" and dt.returncode == 0 and res["drat_trim"]["says_verified"]
144|    res["events"] = events
145|    res["checked"] = unsat and res["drat_trim_verified"]
146|    return res
147|
148|
149|def _kissat_cmd(real, cnf, prf, limit):
150|    return [KISSAT, "-q", "-f", "--seed=1", "--time=%d" % int(limit), cnf, prf]
151|
152|
153|def _cadical_cmd(real, cnf, prf, limit):
154|    return [CADICAL, "-q", "-t", str(int(limit)), cnf, prf]
155|
156|
157|def kissat_drat(cnf_bytes, wdir, limit, check_budget=MAX_CHECK_BUDGET):
158|    return _drat_stream(cnf_bytes, wdir, limit, check_budget, _kissat_cmd, "kissat")
159|
160|
161|def cadical_drat(cnf_bytes, wdir, limit, check_budget=MAX_CHECK_BUDGET):
162|    return _drat_stream(cnf_bytes, wdir, limit, check_budget, _cadical_cmd, "cadical")
163|
164|
165|def cnf_bytes_of(F):
166|    parts = ["p cnf %d %d\n" % (F.nv, len(F.clauses))]
167|    parts.extend(" ".join(map(str, cl)) + " 0\n" for cl in F.clauses)
168|    return "".join(parts).encode()
