===== FILE lane/src/run_proofpass.py (sha256 f2f291677261437e56335ea0fd3e6d8e67a4aa30b633daa04fd408f55f6f78ec, 98 lines; shown 1-62) =====
[... lines 1-62 ...]
1|#!/usr/bin/env python3
2|"""Proof pass for cases already classified UNSAT without proof (pass 1).
3|For each such case: check the stored CNF still has the pass-1 SHA-256, re-run kissat 4.0.4
4|(--seed=1) writing a binary DRAT proof, then verify it with drat-trim while streaming the
5|emitted LRAT through a named pipe into lrat-check (LRAT is not stored; it is reproducible
6|from CNF + DRAT with the same drat-trim build).
7|Usage: run_proofpass.py CASEDIR WORKERS [TAG_PREFIX ...] ; resumable (proofcheck/<tag>.json).
8|"""
9|import concurrent.futures as cf
10|import hashlib
11|import json
12|import os
13|import subprocess
14|import sys
15|import time
16|
17|HERE = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
18|LANE = os.path.dirname(HERE)
19|KISSAT = "/opt/homebrew/bin/kissat"
20|DT = os.path.join(LANE, "tools", "drat-trim", "drat-trim")
21|LC = os.path.join(LANE, "tools", "drat-trim", "lrat-check")
22|
23|
24|def sha(path):
25|    h = hashlib.sha256()
26|    with open(path, "rb") as f:
27|        for chunk in iter(lambda: f.read(1 << 20), b""):
28|            h.update(chunk)
29|    return h.hexdigest()
30|
31|
32|def one(casedir, tag, wid):
33|    res = json.load(open(os.path.join(casedir, "results", tag + ".json")))
34|    cnf = os.path.join(LANE, res["cnf"])
35|    out = {"tag": tag, "pass1_status": res["status"], "cnf": res["cnf"]}
36|    out["cnf_sha256"] = sha(cnf)
37|    out["cnf_matches_pass1"] = out["cnf_sha256"] == res["cnf_sha256"]
38|    drat = os.path.join(casedir, "proof", tag + ".drat")
39|    t0 = time.time()
40|    p = subprocess.run([KISSAT, "-q", "--seed=1", cnf, drat], capture_output=True, text=True)
41|    out["kissat_rc"] = p.returncode
42|    out["solve_seconds"] = round(time.time() - t0, 2)
43|    out["drat_sha256"] = sha(drat)
44|    out["drat_bytes"] = os.path.getsize(drat)
45|    fifo = os.path.join(casedir, "fifo", "w%d.lrat" % wid)
46|    if not os.path.exists(fifo):
47|        os.mkfifo(fifo)
48|    t0 = time.time()
49|    lc = subprocess.Popen([LC, cnf, fifo], stdout=subprocess.PIPE, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT, text=True)
50|    dt = subprocess.run([DT, cnf, drat, "-L", fifo, "-t", "20000"], capture_output=True, text=True)
51|    lc_out, _ = lc.communicate()
52|    out["check_seconds"] = round(time.time() - t0, 2)
53|    out["drat_trim_verified"] = "s VERIFIED" in dt.stdout
54|    out["drat_trim_tail"] = dt.stdout.strip().splitlines()[-2:]
55|    out["lrat_check_rc"] = lc.returncode
56|    out["lrat_verified"] = lc.returncode == 0 and "c VERIFIED" in lc_out.splitlines()
57|    out["ok"] = (out["cnf_matches_pass1"] and out["kissat_rc"] == 20 and out["drat_trim_verified"]
58|                 and out["lrat_verified"])
59|    json.dump(out, open(os.path.join(casedir, "proofcheck", tag + ".json"), "w"), indent=1)
60|    return out
61|

===== FILE lane/src/check_proofs.py (sha256 69c5b76d5103dcbb2042ce12c83140621185d24163bb769193016b00d4805d93, 84 lines; shown 1-58) =====

1|#!/usr/bin/env python3
2|"""Check kissat DRAT proofs of UNSAT cases: drat-trim (backward check, emits LRAT) then
3|lrat-check on the emitted LRAT. Both binaries are compiled lane-locally from
4|marijnheule/drat-trim (tools/drat-trim, commit in COMMIT.txt).
5|Usage: check_proofs.py CASEDIR WORKERS [TAG_PREFIX ...]; resumable (results/<tag>.check.json).
6|"""
7|import concurrent.futures as cf
8|import hashlib
9|import json
10|import os
11|import subprocess
12|import sys
13|import time
14|
15|HERE = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
16|LANE = os.path.dirname(HERE)
17|DT = os.path.join(LANE, "tools", "drat-trim", "drat-trim")
18|LC = os.path.join(LANE, "tools", "drat-trim", "lrat-check")
19|
20|
21|def sha(path):
22|    h = hashlib.sha256()
23|    with open(path, "rb") as f:
24|        for chunk in iter(lambda: f.read(1 << 20), b""):
25|            h.update(chunk)
26|    return h.hexdigest()
27|
28|
29|def check(casedir, tag):
30|    res = json.load(open(os.path.join(casedir, "results", tag + ".json")))
31|    cnf = os.path.join(LANE, res["cnf"])
32|    proof = os.path.join(LANE, res["proof"])
33|    lrat = os.path.join(casedir, "proof", tag + ".lrat")
34|    out = {"tag": tag, "cnf_sha256_now": sha(cnf), "cnf_sha256_at_solve": res["cnf_sha256"],
35|           "proof_sha256": sha(proof), "proof_bytes": os.path.getsize(proof)}
36|    t0 = time.time()
37|    p = subprocess.run([DT, cnf, proof, "-L", lrat, "-t", "50000"], capture_output=True, text=True)
38|    out["drat_trim_seconds"] = round(time.time() - t0, 2)
39|    out["drat_trim_rc"] = p.returncode
40|    out["drat_trim_verified"] = "s VERIFIED" in p.stdout
41|    out["drat_trim_tail"] = p.stdout.strip().splitlines()[-3:]
42|    if out["drat_trim_verified"] and os.path.exists(lrat):
43|        t0 = time.time()
44|        q = subprocess.run([LC, cnf, lrat], capture_output=True, text=True)
45|        out["lrat_check_seconds"] = round(time.time() - t0, 2)
46|        out["lrat_check_rc"] = q.returncode
47|        out["lrat_bytes"] = os.path.getsize(lrat)
48|        out["lrat_sha256"] = sha(lrat)
49|        out["lrat_check_tail"] = (q.stdout + q.stderr).strip().splitlines()[-3:]
50|        out["lrat_verified"] = q.returncode == 0 and "c VERIFIED" in q.stdout.splitlines()
51|    else:
52|        out["lrat_verified"] = False
53|    out["ok"] = (out["cnf_sha256_now"] == out["cnf_sha256_at_solve"] and out["drat_trim_verified"]
54|                 and out["lrat_verified"])
55|    json.dump(out, open(os.path.join(casedir, "results", tag + ".check.json"), "w"), indent=1)
56|    return out
57|

13|Suppose a valid array in this skeleton existed. Each balanced P0 column contributes

25|    t(r)+e_1(r)+e_2(r)+e_3(r)=2.
26|
27|For h, t(h)=5, forcing all three e_j(h)=−1: h belongs to the size2 block in each P1 column. In any such column, its partner cannot have nonempty P0 membership, since that row already agrees with h in a P0 column. A remaining partner must be one of the three empty-membership rows, with t=0. But if an empty-membership row were in ANY size2 block, its sum of e-values would be at most−1+1+1=1, contradicting the required sum2. No partner exists. Contradiction. ∎
28|
29|Parent exact arithmetic also enumerated the27 possible e-triples: t=5 permits only(−1,−1,−1); t=0 permits only permutations of(0,1,1). This finite check is an aid to the proof, not its premise.
30|
31|## Integration rule
32|
33|The exact `b=3` case is mathematically excluded by this argument. Until the full bridge reviews return, preserve it as an explicit structural/empty-cover row. It must never inherit a claimed solver/checker invocation, proof hash or `s VERIFIED` status. A different empty enumeration is NOT accepted automatically by this note.
34|

42|## (2) Defects and gaps
43|
44|**FATAL for certification (remediable; does not show any verdict is wrong)**
45|
46|**F1. lrat-check accepts an empty clause without a conflict [CODE].** In `lrat-check.c` the trace is:
47|1. For a size-0 lemma, `pivot = convertLit(0) = 0` (`:137`).
48|2. With no hints, or hints that never conflict, `checkRedundancy` returns SUCCESS (`:89`, `:129-131`).
49|3. With no RAT hints, `start = intro[1]` (`:158`). `intro[1]` is never written (`:237`, `:337`), so it stays 0.
50|4. The branch at `:160-164` therefore returns SUCCESS. The guard "No RAT and no conflict is FAILED" is commented out at `:161`.
51|5. The lemma sets `found_empty_clause` (`:479`) and the run prints `c VERIFIED` (`:489-490`).
52|
53|So a satisfiable CNF with the one-line LRAT `2 0 0` should verify. Separately, hint IDs are not bounds-checked (`:70-73`).
54|
55|Affected records:
56|- all 60 path-c cases;
57|- `ms01_s03171` and `ms09_s00010`, through their CaDiCaL reruns;
58|- the 486 E0 M2' cases;
